150:
151:#: The curves above are expressed in games out of a 17-game season; they are rescaled if the
152:#: league's `weeks` differs, so they stay an availability *rate* rather than a raw count.
153:PRIOR_BASE_WEEKS = 17
154:
155:
156:def _games_for_rank(pos: str, rank: int, curves: Mapping[str, tuple[AvailabilityBin, ...]]) -> float:
157:    """Look up the fitted games-out-of-17 figure for ``pos`` at positional rank ``rank``.
158:
159:    Buckets are contiguous starting at rank 1 with an open-ended (``rank_hi=None``) tail, so
160:    every rank >= 1 matches exactly one bucket; flat extrapolation past the fitted range lives
161:    in that tail bucket, the same convention ``valuation/bonuses.py`` uses for its curves.
162:    """
163:    for rank_lo, rank_hi, games in curves[pos]:
164:        if rank >= rank_lo and (rank_hi is None or rank <= rank_hi):
165:            return games
166:    raise AssertionError(  # pragma: no cover - buckets are contiguous/open-ended by construction
167:        f"rank {rank} matched no bucket in the {pos} availability curve -- the curve table is malformed"
168:    )
169:
170:
171:def expected_games(
172:    pos: str,
173:    override: float | None = None,
174:    *,
175:    rank: int | None = None,
176:    curves: Mapping[str, tuple[AvailabilityBin, ...]] = EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE,
177:    weeks: int = PRIOR_BASE_WEEKS,
178:) -> float:
179:    """Expected games played for a player at ``pos``, conditioned on positional rank.
180:
181:    Args:
182:        pos: position code (``QB``/``RB``/``WR``/``TE``).
183:        override: per-player expected games, e.g. a known suspension or a player already
184:            ruled out for part of the season. Takes precedence over the curve, and does not
185:            need ``rank`` (bypasses the lookup entirely).
186:        rank: 1-based rank within ``pos`` (best = 1), e.g. by projected PPG. Required whenever
187:            ``override`` is not given -- there is no more flat per-position number to fall back
188:            to (see the curve's fitting note above: a QB1 and a QB35 do not share a durability
189:            outlook, and pretending they do is exactly the bug this replaced).
190:        curves: position -> rank-conditional availability buckets, see
191:            :data:`EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`.
192:        weeks: the league's season length; the curve is rescaled to it.
193:
194:    Raises:
195:        ValueError: unknown position with no override, or no ``rank`` given when one is
196:            needed. Guessing a durability curve (or a rank) for a position/player we have not
197:            actually placed would silently distort its baseline.
198:    """
199:    if override is not None:
200:        value = float(override)
201:    else:
202:        key = str(pos).upper()
203:        if key not in curves:
204:            raise ValueError(
205:                f"no expected-games curve for position {pos!r} (have "
206:                f"{sorted(curves)}); pass an override rather than guessing"
207:            )
208:        if rank is None:
209:            raise ValueError(
210:                "expected_games needs `rank` to look up the rank-conditional availability "
211:                "curve (no flat per-position prior exists anymore -- see "
212:                "EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE's fitting note). Pass an override instead if this "
213:                "player's games truly cannot be tied to a rank."
214:            )
215:        if int(rank) < 1:
216:            raise ValueError(f"rank must be >= 1, got {rank}")
217:        games_at_full_season = _games_for_rank(key, int(rank), curves)
218:        value = float(games_at_full_season) * (float(weeks) / float(PRIOR_BASE_WEEKS))
219:    if value < 0:
220:        raise ValueError(f"expected games must be >= 0, got {value}")
221:    return min(value, float(weeks))
222:
223:
224:@dataclass(frozen=True)
225:class PlayerSeason:
226:    """One player's projected season, reduced to what valuation needs.
227:
228:    ``expected_games`` is optional: ``None`` means "look up the rank-conditional availability
229:    curve" (see :func:`resolve_players`/:data:`EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`) -- the player's rank is
230:    derived from ``ppg`` within their position, not supplied here. Anything supplied here
231:    overrides that lookup entirely.
232:    """
233:
234:    player_id: str
235:    pos: str
236:    ppg: float
237:    expected_games: float | None = None
238:    #: Standard deviation of the player's *season total* fantasy points, if known.
239:    sigma_season: float | None = None
240:    #: Standard deviation of PPG, if that is what the projection source gives.
241:    sigma_ppg: float | None = None
242:    name: str = ""
243:

255:    flex_blocks: int
256:    pool_size: int
257:    #: True when the pool is too shallow to cover demand -- the baseline is then the bottom
258:    #: of the pool and is an *overestimate* of replacement quality. Never silently ignored.
259:    pool_exhausted: bool = False

647:def _stats_covered(stat: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:

650:
651:class ImpactEngine:
652:    """Recompute the board with one ``(source, stat[, player])`` rejected, and diff it.
653:
654:    The recomputation walks the SAME path ``validate/board.py`` walks -- ``blend_statlines`` ->
655:    ``score_statline_with_bonus`` -> ``_games_divisor`` -> ``_cap_expected_games_by_curve`` ->
656:    ``compute_draft_values``, calling that module's own helpers rather than reimplementing them.
657:    The baseline is derived the same way and is asserted equal to the board's own ``dv`` in the
658:    test suite, so a drift between this and the board is a red test rather than a wrong column.
659:
660:    ``sigma_ppg`` is carried over unchanged from the baseline season. Dropping a source really
661:    would change the cross-source spread, but the board runs at ``lam=0``, where
662:    ``dv == evob`` and sigma cannot move a value at all -- so recomputing it would add a number
663:    to the page that provably changes nothing.
664:    """
665:
666:    def __init__(self, inputs: ReviewInputs) -> None:
667:        from draftroom.validate import board as board_mod
668:
669:        self._board_mod = board_mod
670:        self.inputs = inputs
671:        self.cfg = inputs.cfg
672:        board = inputs.board
673:        self._seasons: list[PlayerSeason] = list(board.seasons)
674:        self._index = {s.player_id: i for i, s in enumerate(self._seasons)}
675:        # Marc's playing-time overrides have to be carried through BOTH the baseline and every
676:        # counterfactual, or the impact column would lie for an overridden player: the baseline
677:        # seasons already have his figure applied (re-applying it is idempotent, since the
678:        # override is already curve-clamped), but `_rebuild_season` derives expected_games from
679:        # the statline and would silently hand that player back his source's games figure.
680:        self._overrides = dict(getattr(board, "playing_time_overrides", {}) or {})
681:        self._baseline_dv = self._value(self._seasons)
682:        self._baseline_rank = self._ranks(self._baseline_dv)
683:
684:    # -- internals ---------------------------------------------------------
685:
686:    def _value(self, seasons: Sequence[PlayerSeason]):
687:        capped, _ = self._board_mod._cap_expected_games_by_curve(
688:            list(seasons), self.cfg, overrides=self._overrides
689:        )
690:        return compute_draft_values(capped, self.cfg)
691:
692:    @staticmethod
693:    def _ranks(dv_map) -> dict[str, int]:
694:        ordered = sorted(dv_map.values(), key=lambda d: -d.dv)
695:        return {d.player_id: i for i, d in enumerate(ordered, start=1)}
696:
697:    def _rebuild_season(self, pid: str, statline: StatLine) -> PlayerSeason:
698:        base = self._seasons[self._index[pid]]
699:        divisor = self._board_mod._games_divisor(statline, self.cfg)
700:        points = score_statline_with_bonus(
701:            statline.as_dict(),
702:            self.cfg.scoring,
703:            pos=base.pos,
704:            games=divisor,
705:            bonus_schedule=self.inputs.bonus_schedule,
706:            bonus_curves=self.inputs.bonus_curves,
707:        )
708:        return PlayerSeason(
709:            player_id=pid,
710:            pos=base.pos,
711:            ppg=points / divisor,
712:            expected_games=(statline.games if statline.games > 0 else None),
713:            sigma_ppg=base.sigma_ppg,
714:            name=base.name,
715:        )
716:
717:    def _blend(self, pid: str, rejected) -> tuple[StatLine, bool]:
718:        """Blend this player, with ``rejected`` applied ON TOP of the standing decisions.
719:
720:        The union is the whole point. `rejected=()` here means "the board as it is today",
721:        not "the board with nothing ever rejected" -- the latter made the before/after columns
722:        describe undoing Marc's earlier decisions (Codex 2026-08-21 finding 8).
723:        """
724:        pos = self.inputs.pos_of.get(pid) or self._seasons[self._index[pid]].pos
725:        standing = self.inputs.rejections.for_player(pid)
726:        line, _prov = blend_statlines(
727:            self.inputs.by_source_for(pid),
728:            pos=pos,
729:            games_sources=self.inputs.games_sources,
730:            rejected=frozenset(standing) | frozenset(rejected),

1245:def _nearest_unresolved(
1246:    scorer: Callable[[str, str], float] | None,
1247:    norm_name: str,
1248:    pos: str,
1249:    rows: Iterable[Mapping[str, object]],
1250:) -> tuple[str, float] | None:
1251:    if scorer is None:
1252:        return None
1253:    from draftroom.prep.schema import normalize_name
1254:
1255:    best: tuple[str, float] | None = None
1256:    for row in rows:
1257:        if pos and str(row.get("pos", "")).upper() not in ("", pos):
1258:            continue
1259:        other = str(row.get("name", ""))
1260:        score = scorer(norm_name, normalize_name(other))
1261:        if best is None or score > best[1]:
1262:            best = (other, score)
1263:    if best is None or best[1] < 70.0:
1264:        return None
1265:    return best

1272:
1273:def effective_games_by_pid(inputs: ReviewInputs) -> dict[str, tuple[float, float, int]]:
1274:    """``pid -> (games the board actually credited, the healthy-rank curve figure, rank)``.
1275:
1276:    The second number is the load-bearing one. ``EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`` is fitted on availability
1277:    by POSITIONAL RANK over everybody at that rank, so it is precisely the figure for a player
1278:    about whom the pipeline knows nothing player-specific. ``validate/board.py`` then applies
1279:    ``min(source games, curve)``, and leaves ``expected_games`` at ``None`` when no source
1280:    published a games figure -- in which case ``resolve_players`` fills in that same curve value.
1281:
1282:    Either way, **credited == curve means no player-specific discount was applied**. That is the
1283:    whole comparison this detector makes, and both sides of it are numbers the pipeline already
1284:    produced. Rank is 1-based by projected PPG within position, the same convention
1285:    ``_cap_expected_games_by_curve`` and ``resolve_players`` use, so all three agree on who
1286:    "rank 30" is.
1287:    """
1288:    from draftroom.valuation.replacement import expected_games as _curve
1289:
1290:    by_pos: dict[str, list] = {}
1291:    for season in getattr(inputs.board, "seasons", ()):
1292:        by_pos.setdefault(season.pos, []).append(season)
1293:
1294:    out: dict[str, tuple[float, float, int]] = {}
1295:    for pos, group in by_pos.items():
1296:        for rank, season in enumerate(sorted(group, key=lambda s: -s.ppg), start=1):
1297:            curve = float(_curve(pos, rank=rank, weeks=inputs.cfg.weeks))
1298:            credited = float(season.expected_games) if season.expected_games is not None else curve
1299:            out[season.player_id] = (credited, curve, rank)
1300:    return out
1301:
1302:
1303:def _games_text(inputs: ReviewInputs, pid: str) -> str:
1304:    """Each source's own games figure for this player, in words, for the reason sentence."""
1305:    parts = []
1306:    for s in inputs.sources:
1307:        line = inputs.statlines_by_source[s].get(pid)
1308:        if line is None:
1309:            parts.append(f"{s} no row")
1310:        elif line.games <= 0:
1311:            parts.append(f"{s} no games column")
1312:        else:
1313:            parts.append(f"{s} {line.games:.1f}")
1314:    return ", ".join(parts)

1316:
1317:def detect_injury_vs_expected_games(
1318:    inputs: ReviewInputs, *, ranked_only: bool = True
1319:) -> list[Candidate]:
1320:    """A will-not-play designation sitting next to a healthy player's playing time.
1321:
1322:    THE GAP THIS CLOSES. ``injury_status`` is carried on ``live_data.PoolPlayer`` and emitted in
1323:    the server payload, and it **never touches the valuation** -- grep it and it appears in
1324:    ``live_data.py`` (carrying it) and ``server.py`` (the payload) and nowhere else. So whether a
1325:    designation reaches ``expected_games`` depends entirely on whether ESPN happened to price it
1326:    in, which is accidental rather than principled. Measured on the ranked pool 2026-08-20: Alec
1327:    Pierce is on PUP at ADP 70.3 and the board credits him with 15.50 of 17 games, which is
1328:    exactly the rank-conditional availability curve for WR30 -- the figure for a player nothing is
1329:    known about. Ricky Pearsall (IR) came out right only by luck: he is off the board because
1330:    Sleeper happened to zero his stat line, not because anything read his status.
1331:
1332:    WHY THIS IS NOT ACTIONABLE. Every other detector here says "this source's number may be
1333:    wrong", which ``blend_statlines(rejected=...)`` can express. This one says "the playing-time
1334:    assumption for this player looks wrong", which it cannot express at all: no source is at fault
1335:    (ESPN's 17.0 is an ordinary if-healthy projection) and dropping a source would not change an
1336:    availability figure. So ``actionable`` is ``False``, exactly as for
1337:    ``crosswalk_missing_source``, and the reason says the fix is a playing-time override.
1338:    Recording a rejection that provably changes nothing would put noise in the audit trail.
1339:
1340:    NO THRESHOLD IS INVENTED. Nothing here asserts what a PUP designation costs in games -- see
1341:    :data:`NO_EMPIRICAL_DESIGNATION_FIT` for why that is not fittable from this repo's cache and
1342:    is therefore not asserted. The check is a CONSISTENCY comparison between the games the board
1343:    credited and the healthy-rank curve figure, both printed in the reason so Marc judges two
1344:    numbers rather than a verdict. Severity splits on that comparison alone: ``defect`` when the
1345:    discount is exactly zero, ``hygiene`` when some source did price something in and the only
1346:    open question is whether it priced in enough -- which is the question this module has no
1347:    number for.
1348:
1349:    A player already OFF the board is not surfaced: his designation explains that rather than
1350:    contradicting it, and there is no playing-time assumption left to be wrong. Short-term
1351:    game-status tags never fire (see :data:`SHORT_TERM_DESIGNATIONS`).
1352:
1353:    SETTLED PLAYERS DROP OUT. Once Marc has written a playing-time override for a player
1354:    (:mod:`draftroom.valuation.playing_time`), the gap this detector exists to surface is closed
1355:    FOR HIM: the board's games figure is now a human judgement rather than an unexamined
1356:    healthy-rank default, which is the only thing the check was ever complaining about. Handing
1357:    his own decision back as a fresh candidate would be noise, and the ``hygiene`` wording
1358:    below ("a source did price in N games") would be actively wrong -- no source did; he did.
1359:    Those players are counted into :attr:`ReviewQueue.settled_by_override` instead, because a
1360:    suppression nobody can see is indistinguishable from a detector that stopped working.
1361:    """
1362:    games = effective_games_by_pid(inputs)
1363:    weeks = float(inputs.cfg.weeks)
1364:    settled = set(getattr(inputs.board, "applied_playing_time", {}) or {})
1365:
1366:    designated = [
1367:        pid
1368:        for pid in (inputs.adp_of if ranked_only else inputs.pos_of)
1369:        if is_long_term_designation(inputs.injury_status.get(pid))
1370:    ]
1371:    n_discounted = sum(
1372:        1 for pid in designated if pid in games and games[pid][0] < games[pid][1] - 1e-9
1373:    )
1374:    n_off_board = sum(1 for pid in designated if pid not in games)
1375:
1376:    out: list[Candidate] = []
1377:    for pid in sorted(designated, key=lambda p: inputs.adp_of.get(p, 1e9)):
1378:        if pid not in games:
1379:            continue
1380:        if pid in settled:
1381:            continue  # Marc already set this player's games by hand -- see the docstring.
1382:        designation = inputs.designation(pid)
1383:        recognised = suppresses_missing_data(designation)
1384:        credited, curve, rank = games[pid]
1385:        discount = curve - credited
1386:        no_discount = discount <= 1e-9
1387:        name, pos, _team, adp = _meta(inputs, pid)
1388:        adp_text = f"ADP {adp:.1f}" if adp is not None else "unranked"
1389:        per_source = _games_text(inputs, pid)
1390:
1391:        if no_discount:
1392:            severity = SEV_DEFECT
1393:            reason = (
1394:                f"{name} is listed {designation} at {adp_text}, and the board credits him with "
1395:                f"{credited:.2f} of {weeks:.0f} games -- exactly the rank-conditional "
1396:                f"availability curve for {pos}{rank}, which is the figure for a player nothing "
1397:                f"player-specific is known about, so no part of the pipeline discounted him for "
1398:                f"the designation ({per_source}). No source's number is wrong here, so this is "
1399:                f"not a rejection: the fix is a playing-time override. No games-missed figure is "
1400:                f"asserted for {designation} -- see NO_EMPIRICAL_DESIGNATION_FIT."
1401:            )
1402:        else:
1403:            severity = SEV_HYGIENE
1404:            reason = (
1405:                f"{name} is listed {designation} at {adp_text}, and the board credits him with "
1406:                f"{credited:.2f} of {weeks:.0f} games against {curve:.2f} from the healthy-rank "
1407:                f"curve for {pos}{rank}, so a source did price in {discount:.2f} games "
1408:                f"({per_source}). Whether that is ENOUGH for a {designation} designation is the "
1409:                f"question, and this module has no number for it -- see "
1410:                f"NO_EMPIRICAL_DESIGNATION_FIT -- so it is shown rather than judged. Not a "
1411:                f"rejection either way: the fix would be a playing-time override."
1412:            )
1413:        if not recognised:
1414:            reason += (
1415:                f" NOTE: {designation!r} is not in this module's designation vocabulary "
1416:                f"{sorted(LONG_TERM_DESIGNATIONS)}, so it is surfaced on the safe side and is "
1417:                f"NOT allowed to excuse missing data anywhere else."
1418:            )
1419:
1420:        candidate = _candidate(
1421:            inputs,
1422:            source=PLAYING_TIME_PSEUDO_SOURCE,
1423:            stat="games",
1424:            pid=pid,
1425:            detector="injury_vs_expected_games",
1426:            severity=severity,
1427:            reason=reason,
1428:            detail={
1429:                "designation": designation,
1430:                "designation_recognised": recognised,
1431:                "practice_participation": inputs.practice_participation.get(pid),
1432:                "depth_chart_order": inputs.depth_chart_order.get(pid),
1433:                "games_credited_by_board": credited,
1434:                "healthy_rank_curve_games": curve,
1435:                "discount_applied": discount,
1436:                "no_discount_applied": no_discount,
1437:                "positional_rank": rank,
1438:                "league_weeks": weeks,
1439:                "n_designated_in_ranked_pool": len(designated),
1440:                "n_designated_with_any_source_discount": n_discounted,
1441:                "n_designated_off_board": n_off_board,
1442:                "n_designated_settled_by_override": len(settled & set(designated)),
1443:                "empirical_fit": NO_EMPIRICAL_DESIGNATION_FIT,
1444:            },
1445:        )
1446:        shortfall = (
1447:            "the curve value itself" if no_discount else f"{discount:.2f} below the curve"
1448:        )
1449:        out.append(
1450:            replace(
1451:                candidate,
1452:                actionable=False,
1453:                impact=Impact(
1454:                    scope="player",
1455:                    computable=False,
1456:                    note=(
1457:                        "no dv delta: nothing is being dropped. Rejecting a source cannot change "
1458:                        f"an availability figure, and {credited:.2f} of {weeks:.0f} games is "
1459:                        f"{shortfall}. Ranked by ADP among the rows whose impact is not "
1460:                        "expressible as a delta."
1461:                    ),
1462:                ),
1463:            )
1464:        )
1465:    return out

1908:
1909:def _merge_by_key(findings: Sequence[Candidate]) -> list[Candidate]:
1910:    """One row per ``(source, stat, player_id)``, carrying every detector that fired on it.
1911:
1912:    Multiple detectors landing on the same number is the normal case, not an anomaly: a
1913:    FantasySharks passing-touchdown figure is both the odd one out across four sources AND
1914:    outside what its own passing yardage buys at the fitted 2025 rate. Those are two independent
1915:    reasons to look, and exactly ONE decision. Emitting two rows would let Marc keep a number on
1916:    one row and reject it on another.
1917:
1918:    The surviving row takes the strongest severity's identity (see :data:`SEVERITIES`), keeps
1919:    every reason sentence, and nests each detector's numbers under its own name.
1920:    """
1921:    grouped: dict[tuple[str, str, str | None, str], list[Candidate]] = {}
1922:    for c in findings:
1923:        # Non-actionable rows share a decision key by design (see Candidate.row_id), so they are
1924:        # grouped by row id instead -- merging every unresolved FFC row into one would erase
1925:        # the players.
1926:        grouped.setdefault((*c.key, "" if c.actionable else c.player_name), []).append(c)
1927:
1928:    out: list[Candidate] = []
1929:    for group in grouped.values():
1930:        group.sort(key=lambda c: (SEVERITIES.index(c.severity), c.detector))
1931:        primary = group[0]
1932:        if len(group) == 1:
1933:            out.append(primary)
1934:            continue
1935:        detectors = tuple(dict.fromkeys(c.detector for c in group))
1936:        reasons = list(dict.fromkeys(c.reason for c in group))
1937:        detail: dict[str, Mapping[str, object]] = {}
1938:        for c in group:
1939:            detail.update(c.detail)
1940:        out.append(
1941:            replace(
1942:                primary,
1943:                detectors=detectors,
1944:                reason=" ".join(reasons),
1945:                detail=detail,
1946:                actionable=all(c.actionable for c in group),
1947:            )
1948:        )
1949:    return out

1951:
1952:def collect_candidates(
1953:    inputs: ReviewInputs,
1954:    *,
1955:    engine: ImpactEngine | None = None,
1956:    distance_rel_min: float = DEFAULT_DISTANCE_REL_MIN,
1957:    top_adp: int = DEFAULT_TOP_ADP,
1958:    td_quantile: float = 0.95,
1959:    impact_budget: int | None = None,
1960:    include: Sequence[str] | None = None,
1961:) -> ReviewQueue:
1962:    """Run every detector, attach board impact, rank, and report what happened.
1963:
1964:    ``impact_budget=None`` (the default) computes the real board impact for **every** candidate.
1965:    That is affordable because one full revaluation costs about 1.5 ms -- measured, not assumed:
1966:    ``compute_draft_values`` over the 188-player board is ~1.1 ms, and the whole queue of ~1,200
1967:    candidates lands near two seconds. A cap is available anyway for a much wider run: with one
1968:    set, candidates are screened on a CHEAP proxy first (the change in league-scored season
1969:    points for that one player, one blend and one scoring call) and only the top
1970:    ``impact_budget`` are revalued. The proxy and the real column move in the same direction but
1971:    are not identical -- the real one subtracts a positional baseline and multiplies by expected
1972:    games -- so any capped run says so on the page instead of leaving it an implementation
1973:    detail. ``impact_budget=0`` skips the expensive column entirely.
1974:    """
1975:    engine = engine or ImpactEngine(inputs)
1976:    skipped: dict[str, str] = {}
1977:    notes: list[str] = []
1978:
1979:    suppressed: dict[str, int] = {}
1980:    groups: dict[str, Callable[[], list[Candidate]]] = {
1981:        "contamination_constant": lambda: detect_constant_projections(inputs),
1982:        "contamination_zero_statline": lambda: detect_zero_statlines(
1983:            inputs, suppressed=suppressed
1984:        ),
1985:        "crosswalk": lambda: detect_crosswalk_failures(
1986:            inputs, top_adp=top_adp, suppressed=suppressed
1987:        ),
1988:        "distance": lambda: detect_distance(inputs, rel_min=distance_rel_min),
1989:        "envelope": lambda: _envelope_candidates(inputs),
1990:        "td_regression": lambda: _td_candidates(inputs, quantile=td_quantile),
1991:        "injury": lambda: detect_injury_vs_expected_games(inputs),
1992:    }
1993:    assert set(groups) == set(DETECTOR_GROUPS), "DETECTOR_GROUPS is out of step with the code"
1994:
1995:    findings: list[Candidate] = []
1996:    for name, fn in groups.items():
1997:        if include is not None and name not in include:
1998:            skipped[name] = "not requested"
1999:            continue
2000:        try:
2001:            findings.extend(fn())
2002:        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
2003:            skipped[name] = f"missing cached input: {exc}"
2004:            log.warning("detector %s skipped: %s", name, exc)
2005:        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - one broken detector must not empty the queue
2006:            skipped[name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
2007:            log.warning("detector %s failed: %s: %s", name, type(exc).__name__, exc)
2008:
2009:    # Detector counts are counted BEFORE merging, because "how many rows did this detector
2010:    # produce" is the flood question, and it is not the same question as "how many decisions are
2011:    # pending" (which is the merged row count).
2012:    counts_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
2013:    for f in findings:
2014:        counts_by_detector[f.detector] = counts_by_detector.get(f.detector, 0) + 1
2015:    found = _merge_by_key(findings)
2016:
2017:    # With no budget, every candidate gets the real column and the cheap proxy is not needed.
2018:    # With one, the proxy decides who gets it.
2019:    scored: list[tuple[float, Candidate]] = []
2020:    if impact_budget is None:
2021:        scored = [(0.0, c) for c in found]
2022:    else:
2023:        for c in found:
2024:            proxy = 0.0
2025:            if c.player_id is not None and c.source in inputs.sources:
2026:                pair = engine.points_of(c.player_id, c.source, c.stat)
2027:                if pair is not None:
2028:                    proxy = abs(pair[1] - pair[0])
2029:            elif c.player_id is None and c.source in inputs.sources:
2030:                proxy = float("inf")  # source-wide: always worth the full computation
2031:            scored.append((proxy, c))
2032:        scored.sort(key=lambda t: -t[0])
2033:
2034:    out: list[Candidate] = []
2035:    budget = float("inf") if impact_budget is None else impact_budget
2036:    for proxy, c in scored:
2037:        if c.impact is not None:
2038:            # A detector that already knows its impact cannot be recomputed -- the playing-time
2039:            # detector is not about a source's number at all, so there is no rejection to price.
2040:            out.append(c)
2041:            continue
2042:        if c.source not in inputs.sources:
2043:            out.append(
2044:                replace(
2045:                    c,
2046:                    impact=Impact(
2047:                        scope="player" if c.player_id else "source",
2048:                        computable=False,
2049:                        note=(
2050:                            "nothing to recompute: this is a JOIN failure, so the number is "
2051:                            "missing rather than wrong. The fix is a data/overrides.csv entry, "
2052:                            "and until then the player is absent from the board entirely."
2053:                        ),
2054:                    ),
2055:                )
2056:            )
2057:            continue
2058:        if budget <= 0:
2059:            out.append(
2060:                replace(
2061:                    c,
2062:                    impact=Impact(
2063:                        scope="player" if c.player_id else "source",
2064:                        computable=False,
2065:                        note=(
2066:                            f"not revalued: outside the {impact_budget}-row impact budget "
2067:                            f"(cheap proxy {proxy:.1f} season pts). Raise --impact-budget to "
2068:                            "compute it."
2069:                        ),
2070:                    ),
2071:                )
2072:            )
2073:            continue
2074:        budget -= 1
2075:        if c.player_id is None:
2076:            impact = engine.for_source(c.source, c.stat)
2077:        else:
2078:            impact = engine.for_player(c.player_id, c.source, c.stat)
2079:        out.append(replace(c, impact=impact))
2080:
2081:    out.sort(key=queue_sort_key)
2082:
2083:    counts_by_severity: dict[str, int] = {}
2084:    for c in out:
2085:        counts_by_severity[c.severity] = counts_by_severity.get(c.severity, 0) + 1
2086:    flooded = tuple(
2087:        d for d, n in sorted(counts_by_detector.items()) if n > FLOOD_THRESHOLD
2088:    )
2089:    if flooded:
2090:        notes.append(
2091:            "FLOODED detector(s): "
2092:            + ", ".join(f"{d} ({counts_by_detector[d]} rows)" for d in flooded)
2093:            + ". The queue is ranked by board impact, so the rows that cannot move a pick sink "
2094:            "to the bottom -- but a detector producing this many rows is itself the finding."
2095:        )
2096:    if impact_budget is not None and len(out) > impact_budget:
2097:        notes.append(
2098:            f"{len(out) - impact_budget} row(s) beyond the {impact_budget}-row impact budget "
2099:            "carry no computed impact and are marked as such. They were ranked into that budget "
2100:            "on a cheap season-points proxy, not on the board-impact column itself."
2101:        )
2102:
2103:    if len(findings) != len(out):
2104:        notes.append(
2105:            f"{len(findings)} detector findings merged into {len(out)} review rows: more than "
2106:            "one detector fired on the same (source, stat, player). One row per decision key is "
2107:            "deliberate -- two rows would invite two contradictory decisions about one number."
2108:        )
2109:
2110:    on_board = {s.player_id for s in getattr(inputs.board, "seasons", ())}
2111:    off_board_designated = [
2112:        f"{inputs.name_of.get(pid, pid)} ({inputs.designation(pid)}, ADP {inputs.adp_of[pid]:.1f})"
2113:        for pid in sorted(inputs.adp_of, key=lambda p: inputs.adp_of[p])
2114:        if pid not in on_board and suppresses_missing_data(inputs.injury_status.get(pid))
2115:    ]
2116:    if off_board_designated:
2117:        notes.append(
2118:            "designated and OFF the valued board entirely, so carrying no row here at all: "
2119:            + ", ".join(off_board_designated)
2120:            + ". Nothing needs deciding for them -- a source publishing nothing for a player who "
2121:            "will not play is a source being right, and their exclusion from the board is now "
2122:            "EXPLAINED rather than lucky."
2123:        )
2124:
2125:    if suppressed:
2126:        notes.append(
2127:            "suppressed by injury status: "
2128:            + ", ".join(f"{d} ({n} row(s))" for d, n in sorted(suppressed.items()))
2129:            + ". An all-zero statline for a player on IR is a correct projection and a source "
2130:            "declining to publish him is a source being right, so those rows are not "
2131:            "contamination. Each suppressed player is picked up by injury_vs_expected_games "
2132:            "instead, where the finding actually lives."
2133:        )
2134:
2135:    board = inputs.board
2136:    settled_by_override = {
2137:        pid: binding.describe()
2138:        for pid, binding in (getattr(board, "applied_playing_time", {}) or {}).items()
2139:    }
2140:    if settled_by_override:
2141:        notes.append(
2142:            "settled by a manual playing-time override, so carrying no injury row here: "
2143:            + "; ".join(sorted(settled_by_override.values()))
2144:            + ". The board's games figure for these players is now Marc's own judgement rather "
2145:            "than the healthy-rank default, which is the only thing "
2146:            "injury_vs_expected_games was ever complaining about."
2147:        )
2148:
2149:    return ReviewQueue(
2150:        candidates=tuple(out),
2151:        n_findings=len(findings),
2152:        suppressed_by_injury=suppressed,
2153:        settled_by_override=settled_by_override,
2154:        counts_by_detector=counts_by_detector,
2155:        counts_by_severity=counts_by_severity,
2156:        flooded=flooded,
2157:        skipped=skipped,
2158:        board_source=getattr(board, "source", "blend"),
2159:        n_board_players=len(getattr(board, "players", ())),
2160:        sources=inputs.sources,
2161:        notes=tuple(notes),
2162:    )

150:    #: True when disagreement_cv >= DISAGREEMENT_CV_THRESHOLD. A danger signal only -- LOW/False

168:    #: no extra fetch.

170:    #: level, so a per-source dv would not be comparable row to row, whereas season points is

177:    #: ready for the payload (see docs/REVIEW_QUEUE.md). None = nothing was rejected for
178:    #: him. A rejection must ALWAYS be visible on the board -- a value silently different
179:    #: from what the sources imply is exactly what this field exists to prevent.
180:    projection_decisions: tuple[dict[str, str], ...] | None = None
181:    #: Marc's manual playing-time override for this player, when one actually MOVED his
182:    #: expected games (draftroom.valuation.playing_time). None = no override changed anything
183:    #: for him. Like `projection_decisions`, this must ALWAYS be visible on the board: an
184:    #: expected-games figure that came from a human and looks like a model output is precisely
185:    #: the confusion this field exists to prevent. Informational for rendering only -- the
186:    #: number itself was already applied upstream, in the board build.
187:    playing_time: dict[str, object] | None = None

190:def _player_id_for(row: ffc_client.AdpRow) -> str:
191:    """FFC's numeric player_id when present; otherwise a stable name/team/pos key.
192:
193:    The cached payload always carries `player_id` as of the 2026-08-14 verification in
194:    ffc_client.py, but this falls back rather than crashing if a future refresh drops it.
195:    """
196:    if row.player_id is not None:
197:        return str(row.player_id)
198:    return f"{clean_name(row.name)}|{row.team}|{row.pos}"
199:
200:
201:#: ADP assigned to unranked players so they sort after every ranked player without
202:#: needing a separate sort key. Not a real ADP; `is_ranked` is the field to test.
203:UNRANKED_ADP = 999.0
204:#: Wide std_dev for unranked players. They are never recommended (value 0.0), so this only
205:#: keeps the survival math from dividing by a zero spread if it is ever handed one.
206:UNRANKED_STDEV = 50.0
207:
208:
209:def _match_key(name: str, team: str, pos: str) -> str:
210:    """name|team|POSITION. Position was added 2026-08-18 (Codex review): a name+team key can
211:    silently collide (and silently overwrite) if a team ever carries two same-named players at
212:    different positions; with position in the key, `_load_real_board_by_key` can also ASSERT
213:    uniqueness instead of last-writer-wins."""
214:    return f"{clean_name(name)}|{(team or '').strip().upper()}|{(pos or '').strip().upper()}"
215:
216:
217:def _load_real_board_by_key(
218:    source: str = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
219:) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
220:    """The validated real board's per-player values, keyed by name|team|pos for joining onto
221:    this module's FFC-ID-based pool.
222:
223:    ``source`` selects which projection built the board -- one of
224:    :data:`draftroom.validate.board.BOARD_SOURCE_KEYS` (default: the equal-weight 4-source
225:    composite). A single-source key produces a board valued on that source's statline
226:    unmodified, which is what makes the UI's source toggle an honest comparison.
227:
228:    Built from `draftroom.validate.board.build_real_board()` -- the one production path through
229:    the full pipeline (league-scored Sleeper projections + bonus model + availability-capped
230:    games + EVoB + cross-source disagreement). That module's player_id is the crosswalk's
231:    (Sleeper-derived) id, which does NOT match this module's FFC-derived `player_id` (verified
232:    2026-08-18: Josh Allen is FFC id 2885 here, Sleeper/crosswalk id 4984 there) -- name+team+
233:    pos is the key both sides share.
234:
235:    Reads only cached files under data/raw/ (same guarantee as everything else in this module,
236:    and required for draft night). A DATA failure -- a missing cache, a crosswalk hiccup, the
237:    validate/valuation pipeline not being available -- degrades to an EMPTY result, which
238:    callers must treat as "fallback placeholder mode" and surface LOUDLY (the recommendations
239:    served in that mode are not the validated model).
240:
241:    Raises:
242:        ValueError: two board players collapse to the same name|team|pos key. Silent overwrite
243:            here would quietly hand one player another's valuation; per repo convention that is
244:            a hard failure, never a skip.
245:        DecisionsFileError: the adjudicated-decisions file is present but untrustworthy. This is
246:            deliberately NOT degraded to fallback mode. ``build_real_board`` lets it escape on
247:            purpose (see the note there), and catching it here defeated that entirely: a
248:            truncated decisions file turned into placeholder mode, which reads as "the cache is
249:            stale" rather than "your rejections stopped applying" (Codex 2026-08-21 finding 4).
250:    """
251:    try:
252:        from draftroom.validate.board import build_real_board
253:
254:        rb = build_real_board(source=source)
255:    except DecisionsFileError:
256:        # Fail closed, all the way up. See the docstring.
257:        raise
258:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - degrades to fallback mode, surfaced by callers
259:        log.warning(
260:            "REAL BOARD UNAVAILABLE for source=%s (%s): pool will fall back to "
261:            "ADP-placeholder values -- recommendations are NOT the validated model until prep "
262:            "restores the cache", source, exc,
263:        )
264:        return {}
265:
266:    season_by_pid = {s.player_id: s for s in rb.seasons}
267:    out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
268:    collisions: list[str] = []
269:    for bp in rb.players:
270:        d = rb.disagreement.get(bp.player_id)
271:        season = season_by_pid.get(bp.player_id)
272:        sigma_ppg = season.sigma_ppg if season is not None else None
273:        cv: float | None = None
274:        high = False
275:        if d is not None and d.has_disagreement_signal and d.points_mean > 0:
276:            cv = d.points_stdev / d.points_mean
277:            high = cv >= DISAGREEMENT_CV_THRESHOLD
278:        key = _match_key(bp.name, bp.team, bp.pos)
279:        if key in out:
280:            collisions.append(key)
281:            continue
282:        per_source = rb.points_by_source.get(bp.player_id)
283:        out[key] = {
284:            "value": float(bp.dv),
285:            "value_sd": float(bp.dv_sd or 0.0),
286:            "sigma_ppg": sigma_ppg,
287:            "disagreement_cv": cv,
288:            "disagreement_high": high,
289:            # Season points per source (see PoolPlayer.value_by_source). Copied so a caller
290:            # mutating the pool can't reach back into the cached board.
291:            "value_by_source": (dict(per_source) if per_source else None),
292:            "projection_decisions": tuple(
293:                {
294:                    "source": d.source,
295:                    "stat": d.stat,
296:                    "verdict": "reject",
297:                    "reason": d.reason,
298:                    "date": d.date,
299:                    "detector": d.detector,
300:                }
301:                for d in rb.applied_decisions.get(bp.player_id, ())
302:            ) or None,
303:            "playing_time": _playing_time_payload(rb, bp.player_id),
304:        }
305:    if collisions:
306:        raise ValueError(
307:            f"real-board join key collision(s) -- two players share name|team|pos: {collisions}. "
308:            "Refusing to guess which valuation belongs to whom."
309:        )
310:    return out

312:
313:def _playing_time_payload(rb: Any, pid: str) -> dict[str, object] | None:
314:    """The override that MOVED this player's expected games, flattened for the payload.
315:
316:    ``None`` when nothing moved for him. Reads ``applied_playing_time`` rather than
317:    ``playing_time_overrides`` on purpose, and the distinction is the same one the ``REJ`` badge
318:    already makes: an override the availability curve clamped away changed no number, and a
319:    badge on it would point at a decision that did nothing (CLAUDE.md, on badge scoping).
320:
321:    ``was``/``curve``/``clamped`` come along because an upward clamp is the one case where the
322:    board's figure is NOT the number Marc wrote, and he must be able to see that from the row
323:    rather than from a log line.
324:    """
325:    binding = (getattr(rb, "applied_playing_time", {}) or {}).get(pid)
326:    if binding is None:
327:        return None
328:    o = binding.override
329:    return {
330:        "games": binding.now,
331:        "requested_games": o.games,
332:        "was": binding.was,
333:        "curve": binding.curve,
334:        "clamped": binding.clamped,
335:        "reason": o.reason,
336:        "date": o.date,
337:        "designation": o.designation,
338:    }

341:def load_player_pool(
342:    path: str | Path | None = None,
343:    *,
344:    include_unranked: bool = True,
345:    source: str = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
346:) -> list[PoolPlayer]:
347:    """Build the live player pool: ranked ADP players plus the whole rosterable universe.
348:
349:    No network call, ever -- reads `data/raw/ffc/*.json` and `data/raw/sleeper/*.json` off
350:    disk. Draft night runs with wifi off; this must work from whatever prep last cached.
351:
352:    Args:
353:        path: explicit cached FFC payload, for tests.
354:        include_unranked: set False to get only the ADP-ranked players (the pre-2026-08-17
355:            behavior). Kept so tests that assert on the ranked set stay meaningful.
356:        source: which projection values the pool -- one of
357:            :data:`draftroom.validate.board.BOARD_SOURCE_KEYS`. Defaults to the equal-weight
358:            4-source composite (Marc's decision, 2026-08-20). Use
359:            :func:`draftroom.sources.pool_for_source` rather than calling this repeatedly with
360:            different keys -- that module caches one pool per key so the toggle is instant.
361:
362:    Invariant, asserted below: every player the ADP feed knows about survives into the pool.
363:    Widening the universe must never DROP someone who was previously visible.
364:    """
365:    raw = load_latest_raw("ffc") if path is None else __import__("json").loads(
366:        Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
367:    )
368:    rows = ffc_client.parse_adp_rows(raw)
369:    # FFC's ADP feed is generic (it carries K/DEF); this league rosters neither
370:    # (CLAUDE.md: "No kickers. No defenses."), so they never enter the live pool.
371:    skill_rows = [r for r in rows if (r.pos or "").strip().upper() in SKILL_POSITIONS]
372:    rows_sorted = sorted(skill_rows, key=lambda r: r.adp)
373:
374:    # The validated real board's values (dv, dv_sd, disagreement -- see _load_real_board_by_key)
375:    # and Sleeper's own injury/practice-report fields (best-effort, see below) are both keyed by
376:    # name|team|pos so they can enrich BOTH ranked and unranked players from one lookup each,
377:    # built once up front. An empty real board means FALLBACK PLACEHOLDER MODE (loudly logged).
378:    real_by_key = _load_real_board_by_key(source)
379:    fallback_mode = not real_by_key
380:    if fallback_mode:
381:        log.warning(
382:            "POOL IN FALLBACK MODE (source=%s): no real board available; ranked players carry "
383:            "the ADP-derived placeholder value. Do not trust recommendations from this state.",
384:            source,
385:        )
386:
387:    sleeper_meta_by_key: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
388:    universe: dict[str, Any] | None = None
389:    try:
390:        universe = load_latest_raw("sleeper")
391:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a missing cache must not break draft night
392:        log.warning(
393:            "no cached Sleeper universe (%s); pool will have no injury/practice-report data "
394:            "and (if include_unranked) will be ADP-only.", exc,
395:        )
396:    if universe:
397:        for p in universe.values():
398:            if not p or p.get("position") not in SKILL_POSITIONS:
399:                continue
400:            name = p.get("full_name") or f"{p.get('first_name', '')} {p.get('last_name', '')}".strip()
401:            if not name:
402:                continue
403:            key = _match_key(name, p.get("team") or "", p.get("position") or "")
404:            sleeper_meta_by_key.setdefault(key, p)
405:
406:    def _injury_fields(key: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
407:        meta = sleeper_meta_by_key.get(key)
408:        if not meta:
409:            return {"injury_status": None, "practice_participation": None, "depth_chart_order": None}
410:        dco = meta.get("depth_chart_order")
411:        return {
412:            "injury_status": meta.get("injury_status"),
413:            "practice_participation": meta.get("practice_participation"),
414:            "depth_chart_order": int(dco) if isinstance(dco, (int, float)) else None,
415:        }
416:
417:    out: list[PoolPlayer] = []
418:    seen_ids: set[str] = set()
419:    ranked_keys: set[str] = set()
420:    joined = 0
421:    for rank, row in enumerate(rows_sorted, start=1):
422:        pid = _player_id_for(row)

130:
131:def clear_cache() -> None:
132:    """Drop every cached pool. Call after a prep refresh; tests use it for isolation."""
133:    global _SOURCES_CACHE
134:    _POOL_CACHE.clear()
135:    _SOURCES_CACHE = None
136:
137:
138:class SourceUnavailable(RuntimeError):
139:    """This source's pool built, but carries no real board values.
140:
141:    Distinct from ``ValueError`` for an unknown key: the key is fine, the DATA is not. Serving
142:    this pool anyway put ADP placeholders on screen under a real source's name, wrote a
143:    ``source_changed`` event naming that source, and survived a relaunch -- so the log claimed
144:    picks were made against a board that never loaded (Codex 2026-08-21 finding 5).
145:    """
146:
147:
148:def pool_for_source(key: str) -> list[live_data.PoolPlayer]:
149:    """The full player pool (ranked + unranked) valued under ``key``, cached per key.
150:
151:    Raises:
152:        ValueError: unknown key. Falling back to some other source would put a board on screen
153:            that is not the board the label claims -- and a pick would then be recorded against
154:            a projection nobody chose.
155:    """
156:    if key not in SOURCE_KEYS:
157:        raise ValueError(
158:            f"unknown projection source {key!r}; expected one of {list(SOURCE_KEYS)}"
159:        )
160:    cached = _POOL_CACHE.get(key)
161:    if cached is None:
162:        cached = live_data.load_player_pool(source=key)
163:        _POOL_CACHE[key] = cached
164:    return cached
165:
166:
167:def real_values_in(pool: Sequence[live_data.PoolPlayer]) -> int:
168:    """How many ADP-ranked players this pool could actually VALUE. Zero means placeholder mode."""
169:    return sum(1 for p in pool if p.is_ranked and p.value_is_real)
170:
171:
172:def pool_for_source_strict(key: str) -> list[live_data.PoolPlayer]:
173:    """:func:`pool_for_source`, but refuses to hand back a placeholder pool.
174:
175:    Use this on any path that makes a source ACTIVE -- the toggle and the mid-draft resume. The
176:    lenient version stays for :func:`available_sources`, whose entire job is to describe broken
177:    sources without breaking.
178:
179:    Raises:
180:        ValueError: unknown key.
181:        SourceUnavailable: the pool built but valued nothing.
182:    """
183:    pool = pool_for_source(key)
184:    valued = real_values_in(pool)
185:    if valued == 0:
186:        ranked = sum(1 for p in pool if p.is_ranked)
187:        raise SourceUnavailable(
188:            f"source {key!r} valued 0 of {ranked} ranked players -- its board did not join, so "
189:            "the pool is ADP-placeholder fallback. Refusing to serve it under this source's "
190:            "name; run prep to restore the cache."
191:        )
192:    return pool
193:
194:
195:def available_sources() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
196:    """One entry per source: ``{key, label, player_count, total_count, note, available}``.
197:
198:    ``player_count`` is the number of ADP-ranked players this source could actually VALUE (a
199:    real board value joined on), which is the number that answers "how much of the board does
200:    this source cover". ``total_count`` is the whole pool including the roster-only tier that
201:    exists purely so the board can record a name (CLAUDE.md: "The player pool is TWO TIERS").
202:
203:    Never raises, and safe to call at startup: a source that cannot build is reported with
204:    ``player_count`` 0, ``available`` False, and a note carrying the failure, so the UI can grey
205:    it out instead of the whole control disappearing.

274:        self.board_source = "real" if any(p.value_is_real for p in pool) else "placeholder"
275:        self.real_value_count = sum(1 for p in pool if p.value_is_real)
276:        if self.board_source == "placeholder":
277:            log.warning(
278:                "SERVING PLACEHOLDER VALUES: the validated real board was not available at "
279:                "startup. Bookkeeping is unaffected; recommendations are not trustworthy."
280:            )
281:
282:    def switch_source(self, key: str, new_pool: list[live_data.PoolPlayer]) -> None:
283:        """Swap the served pool for a different projection source (plan B1/B2).
284:
285:        Logs a `source_changed` event for the post-draft audit trail (CLAUDE.md: "the record
286:        must show which board a pick was made against"), then rebuilds every derived index via
287:        `_load_pool`. That event is NOT replayed back into DraftState -- the pool lives outside
288:        the event log, so there is nothing for replay to reconstruct from it.
289:
290:        Order matters: the event is appended (and fsync'd) BEFORE any in-memory state moves. The
291:        old order mutated the pool and `active_source` first, so a failed disk write left the
292:        running app serving one source while replay would rebuild another (Codex 2026-08-21
293:        finding 5). The pool is already built and validated by the caller, so nothing between
294:        the append and the swap can fail.
295:        """
296:        self.session.log.append("source_changed", key=key)
297:        self._load_pool(new_pool)
298:        self.active_source = key
299:        self.session._refresh()
300:
301:    @property
302:    def state(self) -> DraftState:
303:        return self.session.state

755:
756:def _last_source_from_log(session: DraftSession) -> str | None:
757:    """The key of the most recent `source_changed` event, or None if the source was never
758:    switched.
759:
760:    Why this exists: `source_changed` was being appended to the log but never read back, so a
761:    relaunch mid-draft (the exact scenario the append-only log is FOR) silently reverted to the
762:    startup board. Marc would have kept drafting against a different valuation than the one he
763:    chose, with only the header toggle to give it away. An event that is written and never
764:    replayed also breaks this repo's core rule that state is a pure function of
765:    (snapshot, events), which is precisely the kind of thing that rots quietly.
766:    """
767:    key: str | None = None
768:    for ev in session.log.events():
769:        if ev.type == "source_changed":
770:            k = ev.payload.get("key")
771:            if isinstance(k, str) and k:
772:                key = k
773:    return key
774:
775:
776:def _resume_pool_for_source(key: str) -> list[live_data.PoolPlayer] | None:
777:    """The pool for a log-resumed source, or None if it cannot be rebuilt.
778:
779:    Returning None is a LOUD fallback, never a silent one: the caller warns and serves the
780:    default board with `active_source` left at the default, so the header never claims to be
781:    showing a source that failed to load.
782:    """
783:    if _sources_mod is None:
784:        log.warning(
785:            "the draft log says source %r was selected, but draftroom.sources is not "
786:            "importable -- serving the startup board instead. Re-select the source once the "
787:            "module is available; picks are unaffected.", key,
788:        )
789:        return None
790:    try:
791:        # STRICT for the same reason as the toggle: resuming a placeholder pool would put the
792:        # header back on the chosen source's name with none of its values behind it, which is
793:        # the failure that looks most like success (Codex 2026-08-21 finding 5).
794:        return _sources_mod.pool_for_source_strict(key)
795:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - defensive, see module docstring
796:        log.warning(
797:            "the draft log says source %r was selected, but its board could not be rebuilt "
798:            "(%s) -- serving the startup board instead. Picks are unaffected.", key, exc,
799:        )
800:        return None

160:    every rank >= 1 matches exactly one bucket; flat extrapolation past the fitted range lives
161:    in that tail bucket, the same convention ``valuation/bonuses.py`` uses for its curves.
162:    """
163:    for rank_lo, rank_hi, games in curves[pos]:
164:        if rank >= rank_lo and (rank_hi is None or rank <= rank_hi):
165:            return games

167:        f"rank {rank} matched no bucket in the {pos} availability curve -- the curve table is malformed"

169:
170:
171:def expected_games(
172:    pos: str,
173:    override: float | None = None,
174:    *,
175:    rank: int | None = None,
176:    curves: Mapping[str, tuple[AvailabilityBin, ...]] = EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE,
177:    weeks: int = PRIOR_BASE_WEEKS,
178:) -> float:
179:    """Expected games played for a player at ``pos``, conditioned on positional rank.
180:
181:    Args:
182:        pos: position code (``QB``/``RB``/``WR``/``TE``).
183:        override: per-player expected games, e.g. a known suspension or a player already
184:            ruled out for part of the season. Takes precedence over the curve, and does not
185:            need ``rank`` (bypasses the lookup entirely).
186:        rank: 1-based rank within ``pos`` (best = 1), e.g. by projected PPG. Required whenever
187:            ``override`` is not given -- there is no more flat per-position number to fall back
188:            to (see the curve's fitting note above: a QB1 and a QB35 do not share a durability
189:            outlook, and pretending they do is exactly the bug this replaced).
190:        curves: position -> rank-conditional availability buckets, see
191:            :data:`EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`.
192:        weeks: the league's season length; the curve is rescaled to it.
193:
194:    Raises:
195:        ValueError: unknown position with no override, or no ``rank`` given when one is
196:            needed. Guessing a durability curve (or a rank) for a position/player we have not
197:            actually placed would silently distort its baseline.
198:    """
199:    if override is not None:
200:        value = float(override)
201:    else:
202:        key = str(pos).upper()
203:        if key not in curves:
204:            raise ValueError(
205:                f"no expected-games curve for position {pos!r} (have "
206:                f"{sorted(curves)}); pass an override rather than guessing"
207:            )
208:        if rank is None:
209:            raise ValueError(
210:                "expected_games needs `rank` to look up the rank-conditional availability "
211:                "curve (no flat per-position prior exists anymore -- see "
212:                "EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE's fitting note). Pass an override instead if this "
213:                "player's games truly cannot be tied to a rank."
214:            )
215:        if int(rank) < 1:
216:            raise ValueError(f"rank must be >= 1, got {rank}")
217:        games_at_full_season = _games_for_rank(key, int(rank), curves)
218:        value = float(games_at_full_season) * (float(weeks) / float(PRIOR_BASE_WEEKS))
219:    if value < 0:
220:        raise ValueError(f"expected games must be >= 0, got {value}")
221:    return min(value, float(weeks))
222:

224:@dataclass(frozen=True)
225:class PlayerSeason:
226:    """One player's projected season, reduced to what valuation needs.
227:
228:    ``expected_games`` is optional: ``None`` means "look up the rank-conditional availability
229:    curve" (see :func:`resolve_players`/:data:`EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`) -- the player's rank is
230:    derived from ``ppg`` within their position, not supplied here. Anything supplied here
231:    overrides that lookup entirely.
232:    """
233:
234:    player_id: str
235:    pos: str
236:    ppg: float
237:    expected_games: float | None = None
238:    #: Standard deviation of the player's *season total* fantasy points, if known.
239:    sigma_season: float | None = None
240:    #: Standard deviation of PPG, if that is what the projection source gives.
241:    sigma_ppg: float | None = None
242:    name: str = ""
243:

246:class ReplacementInfo:
247:    """Replacement level at one position, with the inputs that produced it."""
248:

251:    baseline_ppg: float
252:    man_games_demand: float
253:    base_man_games: float
254:    flex_man_games: float
255:    flex_blocks: int
256:    pool_size: int
257:    #: True when the pool is too shallow to cover demand -- the baseline is then the bottom
258:    #: of the pool and is an *overestimate* of replacement quality. Never silently ignored.
259:    pool_exhausted: bool = False
260:
261:    @property

263:        """The 1-based ranks whose PPG was averaged into ``baseline_ppg``."""
264:        return tuple(
265:            r for r in (self.baseline_rank - 1, self.baseline_rank, self.baseline_rank + 1)
266:            if 1 <= r <= self.pool_size
267:        )

285:
286:def resolve_players(
287:    players: Iterable[Any],
288:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
289:    *,
290:    curves: Mapping[str, tuple[AvailabilityBin, ...]] = EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE,
291:) -> tuple[PlayerSeason, ...]:
292:    """Normalize any player-ish records into :class:`PlayerSeason` with games filled in.
293:
294:    Accepts :class:`PlayerSeason` instances, plain dicts, or any object exposing
295:    ``player_id`` / ``pos`` / ``ppg``.
296:
297:    Games are rank-conditional now (see :data:`EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`), so a player with no
298:    explicit ``expected_games`` override needs a rank first: this groups by position and sorts
299:    by PPG descending (best = rank 1) -- the same "how good do we think this player is right
300:    now" signal the rest of the pipeline already ranks on -- before looking up each player's
301:    games in the curve. A player already known to be out part of the season should carry an
302:    explicit ``expected_games`` override instead, which skips the rank lookup entirely.
303:    """
304:    coerced = [_coerce_player(raw) for raw in players]
305:    by_pos: dict[str, list[PlayerSeason]] = {}
306:    for p in coerced:
307:        by_pos.setdefault(str(p.pos).upper(), []).append(p)
308:
309:    out: list[PlayerSeason] = []
310:    for pos, group in by_pos.items():
311:        ranked = sorted(group, key=lambda p: (-p.ppg, p.player_id))
312:        for rank, p in enumerate(ranked, start=1):
313:            games = expected_games(pos, p.expected_games, rank=rank, curves=curves, weeks=cfg.weeks)
314:            out.append(_dc_replace(p, pos=pos, expected_games=games))
315:    return tuple(out)
316:
317:
318:def _coerce_player(raw: Any) -> PlayerSeason:
319:    if isinstance(raw, PlayerSeason):
320:        return raw
321:    if isinstance(raw, Mapping):
322:        try:
323:            return PlayerSeason(
324:                player_id=str(raw["player_id"]),
325:                pos=str(raw["pos"]),
326:                ppg=float(raw["ppg"]),
327:                expected_games=_opt_float(raw.get("expected_games")),
328:                sigma_season=_opt_float(raw.get("sigma_season")),
329:                sigma_ppg=_opt_float(raw.get("sigma_ppg")),
330:                name=str(raw.get("name", "")),

650:
651:class ImpactEngine:
652:    """Recompute the board with one ``(source, stat[, player])`` rejected, and diff it.
653:
654:    The recomputation walks the SAME path ``validate/board.py`` walks -- ``blend_statlines`` ->
655:    ``score_statline_with_bonus`` -> ``_games_divisor`` -> ``_cap_expected_games_by_curve`` ->
656:    ``compute_draft_values``, calling that module's own helpers rather than reimplementing them.
657:    The baseline is derived the same way and is asserted equal to the board's own ``dv`` in the
658:    test suite, so a drift between this and the board is a red test rather than a wrong column.
659:
660:    ``sigma_ppg`` is carried over unchanged from the baseline season. Dropping a source really
661:    would change the cross-source spread, but the board runs at ``lam=0``, where
662:    ``dv == evob`` and sigma cannot move a value at all -- so recomputing it would add a number
663:    to the page that provably changes nothing.
664:    """
665:
666:    def __init__(self, inputs: ReviewInputs) -> None:
667:        from draftroom.validate import board as board_mod
668:
669:        self._board_mod = board_mod
670:        self.inputs = inputs
671:        self.cfg = inputs.cfg
672:        board = inputs.board
673:        self._seasons: list[PlayerSeason] = list(board.seasons)
674:        self._index = {s.player_id: i for i, s in enumerate(self._seasons)}
675:        # Marc's playing-time overrides have to be carried through BOTH the baseline and every
676:        # counterfactual, or the impact column would lie for an overridden player: the baseline
677:        # seasons already have his figure applied (re-applying it is idempotent, since the
678:        # override is already curve-clamped), but `_rebuild_season` derives expected_games from
679:        # the statline and would silently hand that player back his source's games figure.
680:        self._overrides = dict(getattr(board, "playing_time_overrides", {}) or {})
681:        self._baseline_dv = self._value(self._seasons)
682:        self._baseline_rank = self._ranks(self._baseline_dv)
683:
684:    # -- internals ---------------------------------------------------------
685:
686:    def _value(self, seasons: Sequence[PlayerSeason]):
687:        capped, _ = self._board_mod._cap_expected_games_by_curve(
688:            list(seasons), self.cfg, overrides=self._overrides
689:        )
690:        return compute_draft_values(capped, self.cfg)
691:
692:    @staticmethod
693:    def _ranks(dv_map) -> dict[str, int]:
694:        ordered = sorted(dv_map.values(), key=lambda d: -d.dv)
695:        return {d.player_id: i for i, d in enumerate(ordered, start=1)}
696:
697:    def _rebuild_season(self, pid: str, statline: StatLine) -> PlayerSeason:
698:        base = self._seasons[self._index[pid]]
699:        divisor = self._board_mod._games_divisor(statline, self.cfg)
700:        points = score_statline_with_bonus(
701:            statline.as_dict(),
702:            self.cfg.scoring,
703:            pos=base.pos,
704:            games=divisor,
705:            bonus_schedule=self.inputs.bonus_schedule,
706:            bonus_curves=self.inputs.bonus_curves,
707:        )
708:        return PlayerSeason(
709:            player_id=pid,
710:            pos=base.pos,
711:            ppg=points / divisor,
712:            expected_games=(statline.games if statline.games > 0 else None),
713:            sigma_ppg=base.sigma_ppg,
714:            name=base.name,
715:        )
716:
717:    def _blend(self, pid: str, rejected) -> tuple[StatLine, bool]:
718:        """Blend this player, with ``rejected`` applied ON TOP of the standing decisions.
719:
720:        The union is the whole point. `rejected=()` here means "the board as it is today",
721:        not "the board with nothing ever rejected" -- the latter made the before/after columns
722:        describe undoing Marc's earlier decisions (Codex 2026-08-21 finding 8).
723:        """
724:        pos = self.inputs.pos_of.get(pid) or self._seasons[self._index[pid]].pos
725:        standing = self.inputs.rejections.for_player(pid)
726:        line, _prov = blend_statlines(
727:            self.inputs.by_source_for(pid),
728:            pos=pos,
729:            games_sources=self.inputs.games_sources,
730:            rejected=frozenset(standing) | frozenset(rejected),
731:        )
732:        return line, self._board_mod._has_projection(line)
733:
734:    def points_of(self, pid: str, source: str, stat: str) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
735:        """``(season points before, after)`` for one candidate. The cheap pre-rank.
736:
737:        Costs one blend and one scoring call, against ~1.1 ms for a full board revaluation, so
738:        it is what a few thousand candidates get screened with before the expensive column is
739:        computed for the ones that survive.
740:        """
741:        if pid not in self._index:
742:            return None
743:        rejected = frozenset((source, s) for s in _stats_covered(stat))
744:        before, _ = self._blend(pid, ())
745:        after, ok = self._blend(pid, rejected)
746:        base = self._seasons[self._index[pid]]
747:        p_before = score_statline_with_bonus(
748:            before.as_dict(), self.cfg.scoring, pos=base.pos,
749:            games=self._board_mod._games_divisor(before, self.cfg),
750:            bonus_schedule=self.inputs.bonus_schedule, bonus_curves=self.inputs.bonus_curves,
751:        )
752:        if not ok:
753:            return (p_before, 0.0)
754:        p_after = score_statline_with_bonus(
755:            after.as_dict(), self.cfg.scoring, pos=base.pos,
756:            games=self._board_mod._games_divisor(after, self.cfg),
757:            bonus_schedule=self.inputs.bonus_schedule, bonus_curves=self.inputs.bonus_curves,
758:        )
759:        return (p_before, p_after)
760:
761:    # -- the public column -------------------------------------------------
762:
763:    def for_player(self, pid: str, source: str, stat: str) -> Impact:
764:        """Board impact of rejecting ``(source, stat)`` for this one player."""
765:        if pid not in self._index:
766:            return Impact(
767:                scope="player",
768:                computable=False,
769:                note=(
770:                    "not on the valued board, so there is no value to move -- which is itself "
771:                    "the finding: a player the board cannot see cannot be drafted against it"
772:                ),
773:            )
774:        base = self._seasons[self._index[pid]]
775:        rejected = frozenset((source, s) for s in _stats_covered(stat))
776:        after_line, still_projected = self._blend(pid, rejected)
777:
778:        dv_before = self._baseline_dv[pid].dv
779:        rank_before = self._baseline_rank[pid]
780:        pair = self.points_of(pid, source, stat)
781:        points_before = pair[0] if pair else None
782:
783:        if not still_projected:
784:            return Impact(
785:                scope="player",
786:                computable=True,
787:                note=(
788:                    "rejecting this leaves the player with no projection from any source, so he "
789:                    "falls off the board entirely rather than being revalued"
790:                ),
791:                dv_before=dv_before,
792:                dv_after=None,
793:                dv_delta=-dv_before,
794:                ppg_before=base.ppg,
795:                ppg_after=None,
796:                points_before=points_before,
797:                points_after=None,
798:                rank_before=rank_before,
799:                rank_after=None,
800:                drops_from_board=True,
801:                n_players_moved=1,
802:            )
803:
804:        new_season = self._rebuild_season(pid, after_line)
805:        seasons = list(self._seasons)
806:        seasons[self._index[pid]] = new_season
807:        dv_map = self._value(seasons)
808:        ranks = self._ranks(dv_map)
809:        dv_after = dv_map[pid].dv
810:        return Impact(
811:            scope="player",
812:            computable=True,
813:            note="",
814:            dv_before=dv_before,
815:            dv_after=dv_after,
816:            dv_delta=dv_after - dv_before,
817:            ppg_before=base.ppg,
818:            ppg_after=new_season.ppg,
819:            points_before=points_before,
820:            points_after=(pair[1] if pair else None),
821:            rank_before=rank_before,
822:            rank_after=ranks[pid],
823:            n_players_moved=1 if abs(dv_after - dv_before) > 1e-9 else 0,
824:        )
825:
826:    def for_source(self, source: str, stat: str) -> Impact:
827:        """Board impact of rejecting ``(source, stat)`` for EVERY player -- the aggregate."""
828:        rejected = frozenset((source, s) for s in _stats_covered(stat))
829:        seasons: list[PlayerSeason] = []
830:        dropped: list[str] = []
831:        for base in self._seasons:
832:            line, ok = self._blend(base.player_id, rejected)
833:            if not ok:
834:                dropped.append(base.name or base.player_id)
835:                continue
836:            seasons.append(self._rebuild_season(base.player_id, line))
837:        dv_map = self._value(seasons)
838:
839:        moved: list[tuple[float, str]] = []
840:        for base in self._seasons:
841:            before = self._baseline_dv[base.player_id].dv
842:            after = dv_map[base.player_id].dv if base.player_id in dv_map else 0.0
843:            if abs(after - before) > 1e-9:
844:                moved.append((after - before, base.name or base.player_id))
845:        moved.sort(key=lambda t: -abs(t[0]))
846:        worst_delta, worst_name = moved[0] if moved else (0.0, "")
847:        note = ""
848:        if not moved:
849:            note = (
850:                "no value on the board moves: the pipeline already excludes this number, so the "
